^SAMPLE = GSM9698680
!Sample_title = 1313_0001_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - NHDF1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698680
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0001
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_relation = SRA: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/sra?term=SRX33116800
!Sample_supplementary_file_1 = NONE
!Sample_series_id = GSE329184
!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM9698687
!Sample_title = 1313_0008_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - NHLF2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698687
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0008
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

!Sample_title = 1313_0015_Normal - LP - FBS_10% - NHLF_1and2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698694
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0015
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.

!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Novitoclax_0,1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.

The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0028
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0035
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Nocodazole_50ng/mL
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0042
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_title = 1313_0049_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - Navitoclax_1umol/L_48h - NHDF1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698728
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Novitoclax_1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0049
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

^SAMPLE = GSM9698735
!Sample_title = 1313_0056_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - Navitoclax_1umol/L_48h - NHLF2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698735
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Novitoclax_1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0056
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.

Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0069
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Palbociclib_4,5umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0076
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

!Sample_title = 1313_0083_Normal - FBS_10% - Bleomycin_5ug/mL_48h_Navitoclax_1umol/L_48h - NHDF2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698762
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Bleomycin_5ug/mL-Novitoclax_1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0083
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698769

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0090
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Rapamycin_10umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.

For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0103
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Novitoclax_1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0110
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

^SAMPLE = GSM9698796
!Sample_title = 1313_0117_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - Navitoclax_0,1umol/L_48h - NHDF2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698796
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Novitoclax_0,1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0117
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_title = 1313_0124_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - NHDF2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698803
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0124
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.

!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.

Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0144
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Bleomycin_5ug/mL
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0151
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM9698837
!Sample_title = 1313_0158_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - DMSO 0,225%_6d - NHDF2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698837
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Dermal Fibroblast
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-DMSO 0,225%
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0158
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

^SAMPLE = GSM9698844
!Sample_title = 1313_0165_Normal - FBS_10% - Nocodazole_50ng/mL_48h - NHLF1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698844
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Nocodazole_50ng/mL
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0165
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

^SAMPLE = GSM9698851
!Sample_title = 1313_0172_Normal - FBS_10% - Bleomycin_5ug/mL_48h_Navitoclax_0,1umol/L_48h - NHLF1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698851
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Bleomycin_5ug/mL-Novitoclax_0,1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0172
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.

Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0185
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Novitoclax_1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0192
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38

^SAMPLE = GSM9698878
!Sample_title = 1313_0199_Normal - NTC - FBS_10% - Navitoclax_0,1umol/L_48h - NHLF2
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698878
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Novitoclax_0,1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0199
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study

!Sample_library_strategy = RNA-Seq
!Sample_relation = BioSample: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/biosample/SAMN57495494
!Sample_relation = SRA: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/sra?term=SRX33116950
!Sample_supplementary_file_1 = NONE
!Sample_series_id = GSE329184
!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM9698885
!Sample_title = 1313_0206_Normal - FBS_10% - Bleomycin_5ug/mL_48h_Navitoclax_1umol/L_48h - NHLF1
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9698885
!Sample_status = Public on Sep 23 2026
!Sample_submission_date = Apr 27 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Sep 23 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: Fibroblast of Lung
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: FBS_10%-Bleomycin_5ug/mL-Novitoclax_1umol/L
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = see experimental design
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = Dermal fibroblast primary cells are maintained with DMEM/F12 high glucose supplemented with 10% FBS and 1% P/S in humidified atmosphere with 5% CO2 at 37C. For Lung fibroblast, media is additionally supplemted with 1X NEAA
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA from each well was extracted using RNeasy 96 Kit (QIAGEN, 74181), combining two technical replicates as starting materials.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = All total RNA samples had a RIN >7.5. Total RNA input of 100ng was employed for library preparation with the NEBNext UltraExpress RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina (NEB, #E3330), together with the NEBNext Poly(A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module (NEB, #E7490) and NEBNext Multiplex Oligos for Illumina (NEB, #E7600) as per manufacturer’s instructions on a Biomek i7 (Beckman Coulter). For double-stranded cDNA purification, Ampure XP beads (Beckman Coulter) were used instead of the recommended SPRIselect Beads. Libraries were amplified with 13 PCR cycles.  The final RNA-seq libraries were eluted in 16 µL EB Buffer (Qiagen) and were quantified by the High Sensitivity dsDNA Quanti-iT Assay Kit (ThermoFisher) on a Synergy HTX (BioTek). Libraries were assessed for size distribution and adapter dimer presence (<0.5%) by the High Sensitivity Small Fragment DNF-477 Kit on a 96-channel Fragment Analyzer (Agilent). Libraries were normalized on the MicroLab STAR (Hamilton), pooled and sequenced on a NovaSeq 6000 (Illumina) with dual index, paired-end reads at 2 x 100 bp length (Rd1: 101bp, Rd2: 8bp, Rd3: 8bp, Rd4:101bp) with an average sequencing depth of ~32 million Pass-Filter reads per sample.
!Sample_description = Library name: Sample 0206
!Sample_data_processing = Sequencing reads were processed with a Nextflow pipeline building upon the implementation of the ENCODE' “Long RNA-seq” pipeline: Filtered reads were mapped against the Homo sapiens (human) genome hg38d1/GRCh38 (primary assembly, excluding alternate contigs) using the STAR (v2.5.2b) aligner allowing for soft clipping of adapter sequences. For quantification, transcript annotation files from Ensembl version 86 were used, which corresponds to GENCODE 25. Samples were quantified with the above annotations using featureCounts (v1.5.1). Quality controls were implemented using FastQC (v0.11.5), picardmetrics (v0.2.4), and dupRadar (v1.2.2) at the respective steps.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38d1/GRCh38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: included_samples_counts.csv Counts for all samples included in the study
